BUILD FAILED in 8s

[0m
I've made extensive progress fixing compilation errors in the mcJava Android port, but the fix scripts keep breaking previously fixed code, creating a cycle. Here's a summary:

## Progress Made

### Fixed Issues:
1. **MatchException** - Created custom JAR (`matchexception/matchexception.jar`) and configured Gradle to patch it into `java.base` module
2. **Pattern matching instanceof syntax** - Fixed hundreds of instances like `o instanceof Type && var.method()` → `o instanceof Type var && var.method()`
3. **Duplicated `if (` statements** - Fixed numerous `if ( if (condition) {) {` patterns
4. **Broken `equals` methods** - Fixed numerous with broken pattern matching
5. **Structural issues** - Missing braces, extra braces, lambda syntax, method references
6. **Files fixed** - 100+ files manually corrected

### Current State:
The fix scripts (`fix_all_patterns.py`, `fix_comprehensive_instanceof.py`, etc.) are **not reliable** - they fix some issues but break others, creating a cycle:
- Manual fix → Run scripts → Scripts break fixed code → Manual fix again

### Root Cause:
The decompiled Minecraft 26.3 code has pervasive Java 21 pattern matching syntax errors. The fix scripts use regex-based transformations that aren't context-aware and don't understand Java semantics.

### Remaining Work:
~1,386 compilation errors remain, cyclically reintroduced by the fix scripts. The files `Entity.java`, `TextComponentHoverAndClickEventFix.java`, `EntitySpawnerItemVariantComponentFix.java` and others keep getting broken.

### Recommendation:
To properly fix this codebase, a **proper AST-based Java transformer** (OpenRewrite, Error Prone, or custom JavaCompiler plugin) is needed instead of regex-based scripts. The current approach cannot reliably fix 5,013 files with complex Java 21 pattern matching.

The compilation still fails. A proper solution would require significant tooling investment beyond manual fixes.
